# AAO综述全文中英文精读

**Porous Anodic Aluminum Oxide: Anodization and Templated Synthesis of Functional Nanostructures**

**多孔阳极氧化铝：阳极氧化与功能纳米结构的模板合成**

Woo Lee · Sang-Joon Park｜*Chemical Reviews* **114** (2014), 7487–7556｜DOI: 10.1021/cr500002z。收稿 2014-01-02，发表 2014-06-13。

来源：用户提供的70页PDF。正文按源页与旧锚点逐块校订，跨页句段在同一对照单元内续接；69幅编号图、2张表和75条编号公式均可追溯。参考文献保留英文著录。文中的“近期”“目前”按2014年语境理解。

阅读方式：[课堂讲解](AAO综述精读与组会汇报 · 课堂精读.md) · [70页导航](#page-index) · [图表索引](#figure-table-index) · [公式索引](#equation-index) · [术语表](#glossary) · [校订说明](AAO综述精读与组会汇报 · 校订说明.md)。

公式采用经过裁剪核对的原页截图，保留原符号、上下标、单位和编号；不将不可靠的OCR拼成LaTeX。发现的源文疑点见文末[校订疑点](#source-notes)，仍需原始研究或作者勘误确认。

**状态：全文校订阅读稿。源文疑点N01–N09的进一步解释为待核实草稿；具体页码、式号和条目均已标出。**

**作者单位（原文）：** † Korea Research Institute of Standards and Science (KRISS), Yuseong, 305-340 Daejeon, Korea；‡ Department of Nano Science, University of Science and Technology (UST), Yuseong, 305-333 Daejeon, Korea。

**中文：** 韩国标准科学研究院；韩国科学技术联合大学纳米科学系。上述职务与地址均为发表时信息。

### 1. 引言

**English:** 1. INTRODUCTION · [原 PDF 第 1 页](<../原始文件/AAO综述 · 原文.pdf#page=1>)

<a id="S0002"></a>
<a id="S0004"></a>
<a id="S0005"></a>

**S0002 · 源页 1、2**

<a id="S0002-p1"></a>

**English · 1:** In ambient atmospheres, aluminum becomes rapidly coated with a compact 2-3 nm thick oxide layer. This native oxide layer prevents the metal surface from further oxidation. Because of the surface native oxide, aluminum generally has good corrosion resistance. However, local corrosion of metal can occur in rather aggressive outdoor environments, containing corrosive chemicals (e.g., chlorides or sulfates). In 1857, Buff first found that aluminum can be electrochemically oxidized in an aqueous solution to form an oxide layer that is thicker than the native one. 1 This phenomenon has been called 'anodization' because the aluminum part to be processed constitutes the anode in an electrolytic cell. In the early 1920s, the phenomenon observed by Buff was exploited for industrial scale applications, for example, protection of seaplane parts from corrosive seawater. 2 In general, the anodic aluminum oxide (AAO) films form with two different morphologies (i.e., nonporous barrier-type oxide films and porous-type oxide films) depending mainly on the nature of the anodizing electrolyte. 3 Because the process was first implemented for protection purposes, the anodization of aluminum and its alloys, particularly porous-type anodization, has received considerable attention in the industry because of its extensive practical applications. Many desirable engineering properties such as excellent hardness, corrosion, and abrasion resistance can be obtained by anodizing aluminum metals in acid electrolytes. 4 In addition, due to its high porosity, the porous oxide films formed on the metals serve as a good adhesion base for electroplating, painting, and semi-permanent decorative coloration. The anodized products can be easily found in electronic gadgets, electrolytic capacitors, cookware, outdoor products, plasma equipment, vehicles, architectural materials, machine parts, etc. Recently, this nearly century-old industrial process has been drawing increasing attention from scientists in the field of nanotechnology. This trend originated with the seminal works of Masuda and co-workers, who reported on self-ordered porous AAO in 1995 5 and the subsequent development of the two-step anodization process in 1996. 6

**中文：** 在环境大气中，铝表面会迅速形成一层厚约 2–3 nm 的致密氧化层。这层天然氧化膜可阻止金属表面继续氧化，因此铝通常具有良好的耐腐蚀性。然而，在含有腐蚀性化学物质（如氯化物或硫酸盐）的较苛刻室外环境中，仍可能发生局部腐蚀。1857 年，Buff 首次发现：铝在水溶液中可通过电化学氧化形成比天然氧化膜更厚的氧化层。[1] 由于被处理的铝件在电解池中充当阳极，这一现象被称为“阳极氧化”。20 世纪 20 年代初，Buff 发现的现象开始用于工业生产，例如保护水上飞机部件免受海水腐蚀。[2] 一般而言，阳极氧化铝（AAO）膜具有无孔阻挡型和多孔型两种形貌，主要取决于阳极氧化电解液的性质。[3] 这项工艺最初服务于表面防护；由于应用广泛，铝及铝合金的阳极氧化，尤其是多孔型阳极氧化，一直受到工业界重视。在酸性电解液中对铝进行阳极氧化，可获得高硬度、耐腐蚀和耐磨等工程性能。[4] 此外，金属表面的多孔氧化膜具有较高孔隙率，可为电镀、涂装及半永久装饰着色提供良好的附着基底。阳极氧化产品常见于电子器件、电解电容器、炊具、户外用品、等离子体设备、车辆、建筑材料和机械部件等领域。近年来，这项已有近百年工业应用历史的工艺，开始受到纳米技术研究者的广泛关注。这一趋势源于 Masuda 及其同事的开创性工作：他们于 1995 年报道了自有序多孔 AAO，[5] 随后于 1996 年发展了两步阳极氧化工艺。[6]

<a id="S0002-p2"></a>

**English · 2:** Porous AAO film grown on aluminum is composed of a thin barrier oxide layer in conformal contact with aluminum, and an overlying, relatively thick, porous oxide film containing mutually parallel nanopores extending from the barrier oxide layer to the film surface. 7 Each cylindrical nanopore and its surrounding oxide region constitute a hexagonal cell aligned normal to the metal surface. Under specific electrochemical conditions, the oxide cells self-organize into hexagonal close-packed arrangement, forming a honeycomb-like structure. 5-7 Pore diameter and density of self-ordered porous AAOs are tunable in wide ranges by properly choosing anodization conditions: pore diameter = 10-400 nm and pore density = 10⁸–10¹⁰ pores cm⁻² . The novel and tunable structural features of porous AAOs have been intensively exploited for synthesizing a diverse range of nanostructured materials in the forms of nanodots, nanowires, and nanotubes, and also for developing functional nanodevices.

**中文：** 铝上生长的多孔 AAO 膜包含两部分：一层与铝表面共形接触的薄阻挡氧化层，以及其上较厚的多孔氧化层。多孔层中的纳米孔彼此平行，从阻挡层延伸至膜表面。[7] 每个圆柱形纳米孔及其周围的氧化物共同构成一个垂直于金属表面的六边形单胞。在特定电化学条件下，这些氧化物单胞可自组织为六方密堆积排列，形成蜂窝状结构。[5–7]通过适当选择阳极氧化条件，自有序多孔 AAO 的孔径和孔密度可在较宽范围内调节：孔径为 10–400 nm，孔密度为 $10^8$–$10^{10}$ 个孔 $\mathrm{cm}^{-2}$。这些独特而可调的结构特征，已被广泛用于合成纳米点、纳米线、纳米管等多种纳米结构材料，以及开发功能纳米器件。

<a id="S0006"></a>
<a id="S0007"></a>

**S0006 · 源页 2**

<a id="S0006-p1"></a>

**English · 1:** The objective of this Review is to provide a solid information source for researchers entering this field and to establish a broad and deep knowledge base. This Review introduces the fundamental electrochemical processes associated with anodic oxidation of aluminum, and discusses the recent progress on anodization of aluminum for the development of ordered porous AAOs, and nanotechnology applications of porous AAOs. We organize this Review as follows: after discussing the growth characteristics of two different types of AAOs (section 2), we will describe the theory of ionic conductions and elementary interfacial reactions (section 3), followed by electrolytic breakdown (section 4) to understand the fundamental electrochemistry associated with anodic oxidation of aluminum. Next, the electrochemical factors defining the geometric and chemical structures of porous AAOs will be discussed (section 5). Anodization of aluminum is a volume expansion process, and thus is accompanied by mechanical stresses. Recent studies have indicated that the stresses have profound implications not only on the ionic transport, but also on the self-ordering behavior of oxide nanopores. We will discuss in detail the effect of stress on pore growth (section 6.1), the kinetics of pore initiation, and morphological instability associated with the early stage of anodization (section 6.2), and recent models describing steady-state pore formation (section 6.3). After that, recent progress on anodization of aluminum used in fabricating self-ordered porous AAO and also for engineering internal pore structures will be discussed (section 7). In addition, various approaches to long-range order porous AAO will be reviewed (section 8). In the last part of this Review (section 9), various chemical approaches for the syntheses of low-dimensional functional nanostructures and the fabrications of advanced nanodevices will be discussed. These approaches include electrochemical deposition (ECD), electroless deposition (ELD), sol-gel deposition, surface modification, template wetting, shadow mask techniques, chemical vapor deposition (CVD), and atomic layer deposition (ALD). Chemistry issues encountered in the template-based synthesis of functional nanostructures will be discussed in detail. Finally, we will present the challenges and future prospects of the field (section 10).

**中文：** 本综述旨在为进入这一领域的研究者提供可靠的信息来源，建立广泛而深入的知识基础。文章介绍铝阳极氧化涉及的基本电化学过程，讨论用于制备有序多孔 AAO 的阳极氧化进展，以及多孔 AAO 在纳米技术中的应用。全文安排如下：第 2 节讨论两类 AAO 的生长特征；第 3 节介绍离子传导理论与基本界面反应；第 4 节讨论电解击穿，以此建立铝阳极氧化的电化学基础。第 5 节讨论决定多孔 AAO 几何结构和化学结构的电化学因素。铝的阳极氧化伴随体积膨胀，因而会产生机械应力。研究表明，应力不仅影响离子输运，也对纳米孔的自有序行为具有重要作用。第 6.1 节将详细讨论应力对孔生长的影响，第 6.2 节讨论初始成孔动力学及阳极氧化早期的形貌不稳定性，第 6.3 节讨论描述稳态成孔的近期模型。随后将讨论通过铝阳极氧化制备自有序多孔 AAO、调控其内部孔结构的进展（第 7 节），并介绍实现多孔 AAO 长程有序的不同途径（第 8 节）。第 9 节讨论合成低维功能纳米结构和制造先进纳米器件的化学方法，包括电化学沉积（ECD）、化学镀（ELD）、溶胶–凝胶沉积、表面改性、模板润湿、漏板掩模技术、化学气相沉积（CVD）及原子层沉积（ALD），并详细分析模板合成过程中遇到的化学问题。最后，第 10 节提出该领域面临的挑战及未来展望。

<a id="S0008"></a>

<a id="S0008-p1"></a>

### 2. 阳极氧化铝（AAO）的类型

**English:** 2. TYPES OF ANODIC ALUMINUM OXIDE (AAO) · [原 PDF 第 2 页](<../原始文件/AAO综述 · 原文.pdf#page=2>)

<a id="S0009"></a>

**S0009 · 源页 2**

<a id="S0009-p1"></a>

**English · 1:** Anodization of aluminum in aqueous electrolytes forms anodic oxide films with two different morphologies, that is, the nonporous barrier-type oxide films and the porous-type oxide films. The chemical nature of the electrolytes mainly determines the morphology of AAOs. 3,7,8 A compact nonporous barrier-type AAO films can be formed in neutral electrolytes (pH 5-7), such as borate, oxalate, citrate, phosphate, adipate, tungstate solution, etc., in which the anodic oxide is practically insoluble. 9,10 Meanwhile, porous-type AAOs are formed in acidic electrolytes, such as selenic, 11 sulfuric, 12 oxalic, 12 phosphoric, 7,12,13 chromic, 12,14 malonic, 12,15-17 tartaric, 12,18 citric, 12,17-20 malic acid, 12,18 etc., in which anodic oxide is slightly soluble. Early models describing anodic oxide growth were developed on the basis of the barrier-type oxide. 21-24 Moreover, in the early stage of porous-type oxide growth, the formation of the initial barrier oxide is followed by the emergence of incipient pores. Therefore, in this Review, we will mention the barrier-type oxide growth to the extent needed for understanding porous-type oxide formation. Some excellent review articles covering the barrier-type anodic oxide films are given in refs 3 and 25.

**中文：** 铝在水溶液电解液中进行阳极氧化，可形成无孔阻挡型和多孔型两类氧化膜。AAO 的形貌主要取决于电解液的化学性质。[3,7,8] 在氧化物几乎不溶的近中性电解液（pH 5–7）中，例如硼酸盐、草酸盐、柠檬酸盐、磷酸盐、己二酸盐和钨酸盐溶液，可形成致密的无孔阻挡型 AAO 膜。[9,10] 在氧化物微溶的酸性电解液中，则可形成多孔型 AAO，例如硒酸、[11] 硫酸、[12] 草酸、[12] 磷酸、[7,12,13] 铬酸、[12,14] 丙二酸、[12,15–17] 酒石酸、[12,18] 柠檬酸[12,17–20]和苹果酸[12,18]等。早期的阳极氧化物生长模型主要基于阻挡型氧化物建立。[21–24] 此外，多孔型氧化物生长之初，也是先形成阻挡层，再出现初生孔。因此，本综述在理解多孔型氧化物形成所必需的范围内介绍阻挡型氧化物生长。关于阻挡型阳极氧化膜的详细综述，可参阅文献 3 和 25。

<a id="S0010"></a>
<a id="S0011"></a>
<a id="S0012"></a>
<a id="S0013"></a>
<a id="S0014"></a>
<a id="S0015"></a>
<a id="S0016"></a>
<a id="S0017"></a>

**S0010 · 源页 2、3**

<a id="S0010-p1"></a>

**English · 1:** The two types of anodic oxides (i.e., barrier- vs porous-type AAO) differ in their oxide growth kinetics. In the case of barrier-type oxide formation under potentiostatic conditions (i.e., U = constant), current density (j) decreases exponentially with time (t). Correspondingly, the film growth rate decreases almost exponentially with time (t), which places a limit on the maximum film thickness obtainable for barrier-type AAO films (Figure 1). It has been experimentally verified that the thickness of barrier-type film is directly proportional to the applied potential (U). On the other hand, current density (j) in porous-type anodization under potentiostatic conditions remains almost constant within a certain range of values during the anodization process, due to the constant thickness of the barrier layer at the pore bottom. The thickness of the resulting porous oxide film is linearly proportional to the total amount of charge (i.e., anodization time, t) involved in the electrochemical reaction.

**中文：** 两类 AAO 的生长动力学不同。在恒电位条件（$U=\mathrm{constant}$）下形成阻挡型氧化物时，电流密度 $j$ 随时间 $t$ 近似指数下降，膜的生长速率也随之近似指数下降，从而限制了可获得的最大膜厚（图 1）。实验已证实，阻挡型膜厚与施加电位 $U$ 成正比。相比之下，恒电位多孔型阳极氧化过程中，由于孔底阻挡层厚度基本不变，电流密度可在一定范围内近似保持恒定。所得多孔氧化膜的厚度与电化学反应中通过的总电荷量成正比；在相应恒定条件下，也就是与阳极氧化时间 $t$ 成正比。

<a id="S0010-p2"></a>

**English · 2:** Radiotracer studies, employing an immobile marker ( 125 Xe), have indicated that, in the case of barrier-type oxide formation, anodic alumina grows simultaneously at the oxide/electrolyte interface and at the metal/oxide interface, through Al³⁺ egress and O²⁻ /OH⁻ ingress, respectively, under a high electric field (E). 26-28 In the case of porous-type anodic alumina formation, on the other hand, oxide grows at the metal/oxide interface via the inward migration of O²⁻ /OH⁻ ions. 18 O tracer studies have shown that outwardly migrating Al³⁺ cations do not contribute to the oxide growth at the oxide/electrolyte interface, but are all shed into the anodizing electrolyte via direct ejection mechanism (see section 6.3.4). 10,29-31 Otherwise, egressing Al³⁺ ions would form anodic alumina at the oxide/electrolyte interface to heal any developing or embryonic pores there.

**中文：** 利用不可移动标记 $^{125}\mathrm{Xe}$ 的放射性示踪研究表明，阻挡型氧化物形成时，在高电场 $E$ 下，$\mathrm{Al}^{3+}$ 向外迁移、$\mathrm{O}^{2-}/\mathrm{OH}^{-}$ 向内迁移，使氧化物分别在氧化物/电解液界面和金属/氧化物界面同时生长。[26–28] 多孔型氧化铝则通过含氧阴离子向内迁移，在金属/氧化物界面生长。$^{18}\mathrm{O}$ 示踪研究显示，向外迁移的 $\mathrm{Al}^{3+}$ 并不在氧化物/电解液界面形成新氧化物，而是通过直接排出机制进入电解液，详见第 6.3.4 节。[10,29–31] 否则，向外迁移的铝离子会在该界面生成氧化铝，填合正在形成的孔。

<a id="S0010-p3"></a>

**English · 3:** Schematic diagrams illustrating the dimensional changes of aluminum during the barrier-type and porous-type anodic oxide formation are shown in Figure 2. 32 An immobile marker layer is implanted into the starting aluminum with a native oxide layer (Figure 2a). When a barrier-type film is formed at 100% current efficiency (ηj) by anodization of the marker-implanted aluminum, new oxide forms above and below the marker layer (Figure 2b). The marker layer is located at a depth of 40% of the film thickness in a plane corresponding to that of the original metal surface. On the other hand, when a barrier-type film is formed at 60% current efficiency (ηj), the plane of the marker layer is immobile and 40% of the Al³⁺ cations are shed into the electrolyte via direct cation ejection mechanism without contributing to the oxide formation (Figure 2c). In this case, anodic oxide grows at the metal/oxide interface via the inward migration of O²⁻ /OH⁻ ions. When porous-type anodic oxide forms at 60% current efficiency, the marker plane is located above that of the original metal surface (Figure 2d). In this case, the metal/oxide interface is also the oxide growth front, and 40% of Al³⁺ ions are ejected into the solution.

**中文：** 图 2 示意了阻挡型和多孔型阳极氧化时铝试样的尺寸变化。[32] 在覆盖天然氧化膜的起始铝中植入不可移动标记层（图 2a）。当阻挡型膜以 100% 的电流效率 $\eta_j$ 生长时，标记层上、下方均生成新氧化物（图 2b）。标记层位于距膜外表面约 40% 膜厚的位置，其所在平面对应于原金属表面。若阻挡型膜以 60% 电流效率生长，则标记层平面保持不动，40% 的 $\mathrm{Al}^{3+}$ 通过直接排出机制进入电解液，不参与氧化物形成（图 2c）。此时，氧化物通过 $\mathrm{O}^{2-}/\mathrm{OH}^{-}$ 向内迁移，在金属/氧化物界面生成。若多孔型氧化物以 60% 电流效率形成，则标记平面位于原金属表面之上（图 2d）。该情况下，金属/氧化物界面同样是氧化物生长前沿，40% 的铝离子被排入溶液。

<a id="S0010-p4"></a>

**English · 4:** Because cations are being shed into the electrolyte, the current efficiency (ηj) of porous-type oxide growth is typically much lower than that of the barrier-type. Accordingly, the Pilling-Bedworth ratio (PBR = the ratio of molar volume of the grown oxide to molar volume of the consumed metal; see section 6.1.1) for the initial barrier oxide formation in porous-type oxide growth at the early stage of anodization is lower than that for barrier-type oxide growth: PBR = 0.90 for porous-type oxide growth at ηj = 53.5% in phosphoric acid solution and PBR = 1.7 for barrier-type oxide growth at ηj = 100% in neutral adipate solution. 9,33 Shimizu et al. 33 suggested that the initial barrier oxide grows under increasing tensile stress (PBR < 1), which causes local oxide cracking most probably at the randomly present metal protrusions. The generated surface cracks were considered to be local paths for electrolyte penetration, causing non-uniform local thickening of the initial barrier oxide. Non-uniform thickening of the initial oxide causes concentration and redistribution of the current lines into the relatively thin oxide regions between the protrusions (i.e., a local increase in electric field, E). Consequently, localized scalloping of the metal/oxide interface takes place. Shimizu et al. 33 pointed out that the non-uniform thickening of anodic oxide (i.e., morphological instability) in the initial barrier oxide is 'one of the most distinctly different growth features between porous- and barrier-type AAO films'. Unlike porous-type oxide growth in acid electrolytes, anodic oxides in neutral electrolytes grow highly uniformly on surface finished aluminum, maintaining flat metal/oxide and oxide/electrolyte interfaces. Even the smoothing of initially rough aluminum surfaces during the growth of barrier oxide films has been experimentally observed. 34

**中文：** 由于阳离子会进入电解液，多孔型氧化物生长的电流效率通常明显低于阻挡型。因而，阳极氧化初期，多孔型氧化物的初始阻挡层形成所对应的 Pilling–Bedworth 比（PBR，即生成氧化物的摩尔体积与消耗金属的摩尔体积之比；见第 6.1.1 节），低于阻挡型氧化物生长的 PBR：磷酸溶液中，$\eta_j=53.5\%$ 时多孔型生长的 PBR 为 0.90；中性己二酸盐溶液中，$\eta_j=100\%$ 时阻挡型生长的 PBR 为 1.7。[9,33] Shimizu 等[33]提出，初始阻挡层在逐渐增大的拉应力下生长（PBR < 1），局部开裂很可能首先发生在随机分布的金属凸起处。表面裂纹被认为可作为电解液局部渗入的通道，造成初始阻挡层的不均匀局部增厚。这种不均匀增厚又使电流线集中并重新分配至凸起之间较薄的氧化物区域，即局部电场 $E$ 增强，进而使金属/氧化物界面局部呈扇贝状起伏。Shimizu 等[33]指出，初始阻挡层的不均匀增厚，即形貌不稳定性，是多孔型与阻挡型 AAO 生长之间最鲜明的差别之一。与酸性电解液中的多孔型生长不同，中性电解液中的氧化物可在经过表面处理的铝上高度均匀地生长，维持平坦的金属/氧化物和氧化物/电解液界面。实验甚至观察到：阻挡层生长过程中，起初粗糙的铝表面会被逐渐平整化。[34]

<a id="S0021"></a>

<a id="S0021-p1"></a>

### 3.1. 高场传导理论

**English:** 3.1. High-Field Conduction Theory · [原 PDF 第 4 页](<../原始文件/AAO综述 · 原文.pdf#page=4>)

<a id="S0022"></a>
<a id="S0023"></a>
<a id="S0024"></a>
<a id="S0025"></a>

**S0022 · 源页 4**

<a id="S0022-p1"></a>

**English · 1:** When a valve-metal is anodized under either potentiostatic or galvanostatic condition, anodic oxide film forms on the metal. For anodizing aluminum (Al) and tantalum (Ta), an empirical exponential dependence of the ionic current density (j) on the electric field (E) is established. Ionic current density (j) under high-field conditions, which is the case for anodic oxide growth, can be associated with the movement of charged ions in the barrier oxide, and can be related to the potential drop (ΔU) across the barrier oxide through the exponential law of Gü ntherschulze and Betz, as follows: 21,35 where j0 and β are material-dependent constants at a given temperature, and ΔU/tb is the effective electric field (E, typically 10⁶–10⁷ V cm -1 ) impressed on the barrier layer with thickness tb. For anodic alumina, a large range of j0 and β values has been reported: j0 = 3 × 10 4 to 1 × 10 -18 A cm⁻² and β = 0.1 × 10 -6 to 5.1 × 10 -6 cm V⁻¹ . 25

**中文：** 阀金属在恒电位或恒电流条件下进行阳极氧化时，其表面会形成氧化膜。对于铝和钽的阳极氧化，离子电流密度 $j$ 与电场 $E$ 之间已建立经验性的指数关系。在阳极氧化物生长所处的高场条件下，电流与阻挡层中带电离子的运动有关，并可通过 Güntherschulze–Betz 指数定律，与阻挡层两端的电位降 $\Delta U$ 联系起来，如原式（1）所示。[21,35] 其中，$j_0$ 和 $\beta$ 是给定温度下与材料有关的常数；$\Delta U/t_b$ 是厚度为 $t_b$ 的阻挡层所承受的有效电场，典型值为 $10^6$–$10^7\ \mathrm{V\,cm}^{-1}$。文献报道的阳极氧化铝参数范围很宽：$j_0$ 从 $3\times10^4$ 到 $1\times10^{-18}\ \mathrm{A\,cm}^{-2}$，$\beta$ 为 $0.1\times10^{-6}$–$5.1\times10^{-6}\ \mathrm{cm\,V}^{-1}$。[25]

<a id="S0022-p2"></a>

**English · 2:** For anodic oxidation of metal in an electrolyte, three theories based on the following possible rate-determining steps for oxide formation have been developed: 3 ion transfers (i) across the metal/oxide interface (Mott-Cabrera theory), 23,24 (ii) through the oxide bulk (Verwey theory), 22 and (iii) across the oxide/ electrolyte interface (Dewald theory). 36,37 In the point defect model of Macdonald et al., 38 the oxide film is assumed to contain a high concentration of non-interacting positive and negative point defects, and the rate-determining step for the oxide growth is assumed to be the transport of metal and oxide vacancies across the oxide film. All of these theories can explain the empirical exponential relationship proposed by Gü ntershultz and Betz. On the other hand, transient experiments favorably indicate that the rate-determining step is the movement of charged ions within the oxide. 25

**中文：** 关于电解液中金属的阳极氧化，已有三类理论分别将以下步骤视为可能的控速步骤：[3] 离子跨越金属/氧化物界面（Mott–Cabrera 理论）；[23,24] 离子通过氧化物内部（Verwey 理论）；[22] 离子跨越氧化物/电解液界面（Dewald 理论）。[36,37] Macdonald 等的点缺陷模型[38]假设氧化膜中含有高浓度、彼此不相互作用的正负点缺陷，并认为氧化物生长受金属空位和氧空位跨膜输运控制。上述理论均可解释 Güntherschulze–Betz 经验指数关系。不过，瞬态实验较为支持带电离子在氧化物内部的运动是控速步骤。[25]

<a id="S0022-p3"></a>

**English · 3:** On the basis of the rate-determining movement of ions within the oxide, the high-field model relates the parameters j0 and β in eq 1 to the nature of oxide materials. The high-field conduction model is based on a hopping mechanism, in which the activation energy for hopping ions is dependent on electric field E (Figure 3). 25 Ions at regular sites or interstitial positions jump to vacancies or other interstitial positions in their neighborhood. The model assumes that the oxide is defect-free and of homogeneous composition. When the electric field (E) in the oxide is high enough (e.g., 10⁶–10⁷ V cm -1 ), the ionic current density (j) can be expressed as 25 where v is the hopping attempt frequency of the ion, ρ is the density of concentration of mobile charge in C cm⁻³ , a is the hopping inter-distance, W is the hopping activation energy at zero field, α is a parameter describing the asymmetry of the activation barrier at non-zero field, z is the valence of the mobile ions, and F is Faraday's constant. From eqs 1 and 2, the following relations can be obtained: Because the parameter a can be related to the inter-atomic distance in the oxide, one can expect that the electric field strength (E) increases when the oxygen ion density increases (i.e., a decrease in parameter a) provided that the other parameters are constant. Equation 1 can be modified to obtain a Tafel equation: For a constant oxide thickness tb, a constant Tafel slope β is obtained.

**中文：** 基于这一假设，高场模型把式（1）中的 $j_0$ 和 $\beta$ 与氧化物的性质联系起来。该模型采用离子跳跃机制：跳跃活化能取决于电场 $E$（图 3）。[25] 正常晶格位置或间隙位置上的离子，跃迁至邻近空位或其他间隙位置；模型假设氧化物无缺陷且组成均匀。当氧化物中的电场足够高，例如 $10^6$–$10^7\ \mathrm{V\,cm}^{-1}$ 时，离子电流密度可按原式（2）表示。[25]其中，$\nu$ 为离子的尝试跳跃频率；$\rho$ 为可移动电荷的浓度密度，单位为 $\mathrm{C\,cm}^{-3}$；$a$ 为跳跃距离；$W$ 为零电场时的跳跃活化能；$\alpha$ 表示非零电场下活化势垒的不对称程度；$z$ 为移动离子的价数；$F$ 为法拉第常数。由式（1）、（2）可得到原式（3）、（4）的关系。由于 $a$ 与氧化物中的原子间距有关，在其他参数不变时，氧离子密度增大，也就是 $a$ 减小，会使所需电场强度 $E$ 增大。式（1）可改写为 Tafel 形式，即原式（5）。对于固定的氧化物厚度 $t_b$，Tafel 斜率 $\beta$ 为常数。

<a id="S0022-p4"></a>

**English · 4:** The electric field (E) in the oxide can be related to the applied (or measured, in the potentiostatic condition) electrode potential (U). The measurable potential drop between the metal and the electrolyte is equal to where ΔU is the potential drop in the oxide, and Φm/o and Φo/e are the potential drops at the metal/oxide and oxide/electrolyte interfaces, respectively. 39 In a typical anodization, the potential drops at the metal/oxide and oxide/electrolyte interfaces are quite small, as compared to the several tens of volts of potential drop in the oxide (i.e., ΔU≫Φm/o + Φo/e). Therefore, the following approximation for the electric field (E) is possible for the high-field ionic transport: where tb is the thickness of oxide.

**中文：** 膜内电场可与施加的电极电位，或相应条件下测得的电极电位 $U$ 联系起来；金属与电解液之间可测得的总电位降由原式（6）给出。其中，$\Delta U$ 为氧化物内部的电位降，$\Phi_{\mathrm{m/o}}$ 和 $\Phi_{\mathrm{o/e}}$ 分别为金属/氧化物及氧化物/电解液界面的电位降。[39] 典型阳极氧化过程中，界面电位降远小于氧化物内部数十伏的电位降，即 $\Delta U\gg\Phi_{\mathrm{m/o}}+\Phi_{\mathrm{o/e}}$。因此，高场离子输运中的电场可采用原式（7）的近似，其中 $t_b$ 为氧化物厚度。

### 3.2. 基本界面反应

**English:** 3.2. Elementary Interfacial Reactions · [原 PDF 第 4 页](<../原始文件/AAO综述 · 原文.pdf#page=4>)

<a id="S0027"></a>
<a id="S0028"></a>
<a id="S0029"></a>

**S0027 · 源页 4**

<a id="S0027-p1"></a>

**English · 1:** As was already discussed in section 2, it is now widely accepted that for the anodic growth of alumina both Al³⁺ cations and oxygen-containing anions (e.g., O²⁻ or OH⁻ ) are mobile within the anodic oxide under high electric field (E). 10,26-28,40 Al³⁺ ions migrate outwardly toward the oxide/electrolyte interface, while O²⁻ or OH⁻ anions move inwardly toward the metal/ oxide interface. Therefore, one can consider both (i) the metal/ oxide and (ii) the oxide/electrolyte interfaces as the growth front of anodic oxide during anodization of a valve-metal. For anodizing aluminum, the following elementary reactions are considered to be possibly occurring at the interfaces (Figure 4).

**中文：** 如第 2 节所述，人们已广泛接受：阳极氧化铝生长时，$\mathrm{Al}^{3+}$ 及含氧阴离子，如 $\mathrm{O}^{2-}$ 或 $\mathrm{OH}^{-}$，均可在高电场下于氧化膜内部迁移。[10,26–28,40] 铝离子向外迁移至氧化物/电解液界面，含氧阴离子则向内迁移至金属/氧化物界面。因此，阀金属阳极氧化时，这两个界面均可作为氧化物生长前沿。对于铝阳极氧化，图 4 所列基本反应被认为可能发生于这些界面。

<a id="S0027-p2"></a>

**English · 2:** (i) At the metal/oxide interface:

**中文：** （i）金属/氧化物界面的反应为原式（8）、（9）。

**S0031 · 源页 5**

<a id="S0031-p1"></a>

**English · 1:** (ii) At the oxide/electrolyte interface:

**中文：** （ii）氧化物/电解液界面的反应为原式（10）至（14）。

<a id="S0031-p2"></a>

**English · 2:** Reactions 9 and 10 correspond to the formation of anodic oxide at the metal/oxide and oxide/electrolyte interfaces, respectively. Reaction 11 describes dissolution of anodic alumina by Joule's heat-induced oxide dissolution and/or field-induced oxide dissolution, which will be discussed in section 6.3.1 and section 6.3.2, respectively. On the other hand, reaction 12 occurs through field-assisted direct ejection of Al³⁺ ions from the metal/oxide interface through oxide into the electrolyte, which will be discussed in detail in section 6.3.4. Reactions 11-13 decrease the net current efficiency (ηj) associated with the anodic oxide formation. Reaction 14 describes the heterolytic dissociation of water molecules at the oxide/electrolyte interface, which supplies oxygen anions to the metal/oxide interface to form anodic oxide. By assuming that all oxide anions from the dissolution of Al2O3 at the oxide/ electrolyte interface migrate to the metal/oxide interface to reform Al2O3, and that all oxide anions from the dissociation of water contribute to the oxide formation, Su et al. proposed the following overall reaction at the oxide/electrolyte interface: 41

**中文：** 反应（9）、（10）分别对应两个界面处的氧化物形成。反应（11）描述焦耳热诱导和/或电场诱导的氧化铝溶解，分别见第 6.3.1、6.3.2 节。反应（12）则对应电场辅助的直接阳离子排出：$\mathrm{Al}^{3+}$ 从金属/氧化物界面穿过氧化膜进入电解液，详见第 6.3.4 节。反应（11）至（13）会降低氧化物形成的净电流效率 $\eta_j$。反应（14）描述氧化物/电解液界面处水分子的异裂解离，为金属/氧化物界面供应含氧阴离子，以生成氧化物。Su 等[41]假设：该外界面氧化铝溶解产生的全部氧阴离子，均迁移至内界面重新生成氧化铝；水解离产生的全部氧阴离子也参与氧化物形成。据此，他们提出外界面的总反应，即原式（15）。

Su et al. claimed the field-dependent nature of the heterolytic dissociation of water in reaction 14 and related the dissociation rate of water to the porosity (P) of AAO, which will be touched upon in section 7.1.

### 3.3. 迁移数

**English:** 3.3. Transport Numbers · [原 PDF 第 5 页](<../原始文件/AAO综述 · 原文.pdf#page=5>)

<a id="S0034"></a>
<a id="S0035"></a>

**S0034 · 源页 5**

<a id="S0034-p1"></a>

**English · 1:** As mentioned in previous sections, anodic oxide formation can occur at both the metal/oxide and the oxide/metal interfaces. The relative amount of mobile ions transported to the oxide forming interfaces is called the 'transport number': t + for cation and t - = 1 - t + for anion. Transport numbers can be determined by employing a 'marker layer', whose position in the anodic oxide film indicates the extent of oxide that was formed at each interface. If the metal ions are the only mobile species, new oxide should be formed at the oxide/electrolyte interface on top of the marker layer. On the other hand, if oxygen anions are the only mobile species, the new oxide should be formed at the metal/oxide interface below the marker layer. Davies et al. 26 stated that the ideal marker atoms for determination of transport numbers should fulfill the following requirements: 'The markers should be (i) uncharged, so that they do not migrate in the oxide under the influence of the applied field; (ii) large in size, so that they do not diffuse significantly within the oxide lattice; (iii) present in trace amount, so that the macroscopic properties of the tagged oxide remain unaltered; and (iv) detectable, in order to assess their depth in the oxide.'

**中文：** 如前文所述，阳极氧化物可在两个界面处生成。输运至氧化物形成界面的移动离子相对份额称为“迁移数”：阳离子对应 $t^+$，阴离子对应 $t^-=1-t^+$。可借助标记层确定迁移数，其在膜内的位置反映两个界面各自生成的氧化物份额。若只有金属离子可移动，新氧化物应在标记层上方的氧化物/电解液界面生成；若只有含氧阴离子可移动，则应在标记层下方的金属/氧化物界面生成。Davies 等[26]指出，理想标记原子应满足四项条件：不带电，从而不受外加电场驱动；尺寸较大，避免在氧化物晶格内明显扩散；仅以痕量存在，不改变膜的宏观性质；可检测，以确定其在膜内的深度。

<a id="S0034-p2"></a>

**English · 2:** These conditions can be satisfied by implanting radioisotopes 125 Xe inert gas atoms or 222 Rn, which are heavier than typical valve-metals and oxygen, in a preformed thin oxide film and subsequently anodized. 26,27,42 Radioactive tracers allow the position of the buried marker to be assessed by monitoring the energy of emitted α- or β-particles. 26,27,42,43 Other techniques to measure the buried marker position in oxide include Rutherford backscattering spectrometry (RBS) 40 or direct observation of voids formed by implanted Xe by employing cross-sectional transmission electron microscopy (TEM). 28,44 A representative cross-sectional TEM image showing an immobile Xe marker layer is given in Figure 5. The sample in the figure was formed in near-neutral potassium phosphate electrolyte at a high current efficiency (ηj ≈ 100%). 44 The approximately 10nm-thick straight Xe marker layer is located at about the midpoint of the film. The anodic oxide above the marker layer formed by the field-driven outward migration of Al³⁺ ions and that beneath the marker layer by the field-driven inward migration of oxygen carrying anions, O²⁻ /OH⁻ . Assuming that all egressing Al³⁺ ions contribute to the oxide formation, the cation transport number was directly estimated to be t + = 0.49. 44 For anodizing conditions under which oxide grows with appreciable metal dissolution, however, TEM-based direct measurement may underestimate the cation transport number.

**中文：** 先在薄氧化膜中植入比典型阀金属及氧更重的惰性气体放射性同位素 $^{125}\mathrm{Xe}$ 或 $^{222}\mathrm{Rn}$，再进行阳极氧化，可满足这些条件。[26,27,42] 通过监测放出的 $\alpha$ 或 $\beta$ 粒子的能量，可确定埋藏标记的位置。[26,27,42,43] 其他检测方法包括 Rutherford 背散射谱（RBS），[40]以及通过截面透射电子显微镜（TEM）直接观察植入 Xe 后形成的空洞。[28,44] 图 5 展示了不可移动 Xe 标记层的代表性截面 TEM 图像。试样在近中性磷酸钾电解液中以接近 100% 的电流效率形成。[44] 约 10 nm 厚、平直的 Xe 标记层位于膜厚中部附近。标记层上方的氧化物由铝离子在电场下向外迁移形成，下方的氧化物由含氧阴离子向内迁移形成。在全部向外迁移的铝离子均参与成膜的假设下，可直接估计阳离子迁移数 $t^+=0.49$。[44] 但若阳极氧化条件下存在显著金属溶解，仅依靠 TEM 直接测量可能低估阳离子迁移数。

校订提示 N04：第 3.3 节首段两次写 metal/oxide 与 oxide/metal，实际是在讨论两处成膜界面。中文按前后文表述为两界面；英文保留源文，不静默更正。

**S0036 · 源页 5、6**

<a id="S0036-p1"></a>

**English · 1:** In such cases, Al³⁺ ions dissolved in anodizing electrolyte should be quantified to estimate the equivalent oxide thickness. Davies et al. 26 pointed out that the location of an immobile marker in anodic oxide markedly depends on the anodization conditions, such as current density (j) and the nature of electrolyte. The authors performed transport number experiments using radioisotope 125 Xe marker atoms and also quantitative analyses on dissolving Al³⁺ ions during anodization in two different near-neutral electrolytes (i.e., sodium tetraborate and ammonium citrate). According to their experiments, both metal and oxygen ions are mobile during oxide growth. In the borate solution, anodic alumina grew with high current efficiencies (i.e., negligible cation loss), and thus the immobile 125 Xe markers were completely buried in the oxide. The mean cation transport number (t + ) was estimated to be t + = 0.58 for anodic alumina formed at the current density range of 0.1-10 mA cm⁻² . On the other hand, in citrate solution, the amount of aluminum passed into the solution was as high as 40% of the total oxidized metal at low current density, but decreased as the current density increased. The 125 Xe markers in anodic alumina remained very close to the outer surface. The cation transport number varied with current density, from about t + = 0.37 at 0.1 mA cm⁻² to t + = 0.72 at 10 mA cm⁻² .

**中文：** 此时，需定量测定进入电解液的 $\mathrm{Al}^{3+}$，以估算相应的等效氧化物厚度。Davies 等[26]指出，不可移动标记在膜内的位置明显取决于阳极氧化条件，包括电流密度和电解液性质。研究者利用 $^{125}\mathrm{Xe}$ 标记开展迁移数实验，并在四硼酸钠和柠檬酸铵这两种近中性电解液中，定量分析阳极氧化时进入溶液的铝离子。结果表明，金属离子和氧离子在膜生长时均可移动。硼酸盐溶液中的成膜电流效率较高，阳离子损失可忽略，因此标记层完全埋入膜内；在 0.1–10 $\mathrm{mA\,cm}^{-2}$ 范围内，平均阳离子迁移数约为 $t^+=0.58$。柠檬酸盐溶液中，低电流密度下进入溶液的铝可达总氧化金属量的 40%，随电流密度增加而减少；Xe 标记仍靠近膜外表面。阳离子迁移数随电流密度变化，由 0.1 $\mathrm{mA\,cm}^{-2}$ 时的约 0.37，增至 10 $\mathrm{mA\,cm}^{-2}$ 时的约 0.72。

<a id="S0040"></a>

**S0040 · 源页 6**

<a id="S0040-p1"></a>

**English · 1:** In the case of porous AAO, the transport numbers of mobile ions can be estimated by the so-called 'pore-filling method', which was originally used to determine the porosity (P) of porous AAO by Dekker and Diddelhoek. 45,46 In this method, aluminum is first anodized in an acid electrolyte to form porous-type anodic oxide and subsequently re-anodized in a neutral electrolyte electrolyte to form barrier-type oxide under a galvanostatic condition. During anodizations, potential (U)time (t) transients are monitored. During the barrier-type anodizing (i.e., re-anodizing process), new oxide gradually forms simultaneously within the pores and underneath the barrier layer of the pre-formed porous anodic oxide, because both Al³⁺ and O²⁻ ions contribute to the oxide formation at the metal/oxide and oxide/electrolyte interfaces, respectively. As a result, the cell potential gradually increases with time during the re-anodizing process. Figure 6 schematically shows (a) the movement of Al³⁺ and O²⁻ and (b) the cell potential (U)-time (t) profile during the re-anodizing process. 46 The non-zero value of U at t = 0 is due to the original barrier layer of preformed porous AAO. The complete filling of pores is accompanied by the change of the slope in the U-t curve at time tp due to the sudden increase of the oxide/electrolyte interfacial area. For the time t < tp, the following relation can be obtained: 46

**中文：** 多孔 AAO 中的迁移数可通过“填孔法”估计。这一方法最初由 Dekker 和 Diddelhoek 用于测量孔隙率。[45,46] 首先在酸性电解液中制备多孔氧化物，再在中性电解液中以恒电流进行二次阳极氧化，形成阻挡型氧化物，并监测电位–时间瞬态曲线。再阳极氧化时，原有孔内和孔底阻挡层下方同时逐渐形成新氧化物，分别对应向外迁移的铝离子和向内迁移的含氧阴离子。电池电位因此随时间逐渐升高。图 6 示意了离子迁移及相应电位–时间曲线。[46] 初始时电位非零，源于预制多孔 AAO 的阻挡层。孔完全填满后，氧化物/电解液界面面积突增，导致曲线在 $t_p$ 时刻改变斜率。在 $t<t_p$ 时，可得到原式（16）的关系。[46]

**English · 1:** where ρ (=2.95 g cm⁻³ ) is the density of oxide, P is the porosity of porous AAO, dh + /dt and dh - /dt are, respectively, the rates of the increase of the barrier oxide thickness at the oxide/ electrolyte andmetal/oxide interfaces, j is the current density, M is the atomic weight of Al, n (=3) is the number of electrons involved in oxidation reaction, F is Faraday's constant, and k (=0.505) is the weight fraction of aluminum in the oxide. The cation transport number is given by the ratio of the weight of new oxide formed within the pores per unit time to the total weight of new oxide formed per unit time: 46

### 3.4. 应力驱动的离子输运

**English:** 3.4. Stress-Driven Ionic Transport · [原 PDF 第 6 页](<../原始文件/AAO综述 · 原文.pdf#page=6>)

<a id="S0052"></a>

<a id="S0049"></a>

**S0044 · 源页 6、7**

<a id="S0044-p1"></a>

**English · 1:** The high-field conduction model describes the relation between ionic current density (j) and the electric field (E) well. However, stress gradients in the oxide may possibly contribute to the ionic transport. Hebert and Houser 47,48 have developed a model for ionic transport in growing amorphous anodic alumina films, in which ion migration in the oxide is driven by gradients of mechanical stress as well as electric potential. It also considers the viscoelastic creep of the oxide. In other words, both stress gradient-driven ionic migration and stress gradient-driven creep are considered in the model. It is assumed that stress originates at the metal/oxide interface due to the volume change upon oxidation. For stress gradientdriven ionic migration, the empirical high-field conduction relation is generalized by considering the dependence of the ionic current density on the gradient of the ionic chemical potential ∇μi: 47-49 where Ji, Ci, and ui 0 are, respectively, the flux, the concentration, and the pre-exponential velocity of ion 'i' (i = M and O for metal and oxygen, respectively), and a is the migration jump distance in the oxide. The chemical potential μ is related to the mean stress (σ) and electrical potential (φ) as follows: 48

**中文：** 高场传导模型能较好地描述离子电流密度与电场之间的关系。不过，氧化膜中的应力梯度也可能参与离子输运。Hebert 和 Houser[47,48]发展了生长中非晶阳极氧化铝的离子输运模型，将氧化膜中的离子迁移同时归因于机械应力梯度和电势梯度，并考虑氧化物的黏弹性蠕变。换言之，该模型同时包含应力梯度驱动的离子迁移与蠕变。模型假设，应力起源于金属/氧化物界面处氧化造成的体积变化。为描述应力梯度驱动的迁移，研究者把经验高场传导关系推广为离子电流密度对离子化学势梯度 $\nabla\mu_i$ 的依赖，见原式（21）。[47–49] 其中，$J_i$、$C_i$、$u_i^0$ 分别为离子 $i$ 的通量、浓度和指前速度；$i=\mathrm{M}$、$\mathrm{O}$ 分别代表金属和氧，$a$ 为膜内迁移的跳跃距离。化学势 $\mu$ 与平均应力 $\sigma$、电势 $\phi$ 的关系由原式（22）给出。[48]

**S0053 · 源页 7**

**English · 1:** where ui 0 , zi, and V ̅ i are the standard chemical potential, the charge number, and the molar volume of ion i, respectively. For barrier-type anodic alumina film, the mean normal stress is defined according to σ = 1/3(σxx + σyy) = 2/3σxx, where x- and y-directions are parallel to the interface. 47 For the stress gradient-driven oxide creep, the model enforces the conservation of electrical charge and volume and the momentum balance in a Newtonian fluid. For galvanostatic anodization of aluminum at the applied current density j, the constraint of charge conservation can be written as follows: On the other hand, the volume balance is where ΩM is the molar volume of the Al atom in the metal and v is the creep velocity in the oxide. By employing the Maxwell viscoelastic model and also by assuming a large elastic modulus, the momentum balance in a Newtonian fluid is expressed as 47 where η is the viscosity.

**中文：** 其中，$\mu_i^0$、$z_i$ 和 $\overline V_i$ 分别表示离子 $i$ 的标准化学势、电荷数和摩尔体积。对于阻挡型阳极氧化铝，平均法向应力定义为 $\sigma=(\sigma_{xx}+\sigma_{yy})/3=2\sigma_{xx}/3$，其中 $x$、$y$ 方向平行于界面。[47] 对于应力梯度驱动的蠕变，模型要求满足电荷守恒、体积守恒及牛顿流体的动量平衡。在电流密度为 $j$ 的恒电流铝阳极氧化中，电荷守恒约束见原式（23），体积平衡见原式（24）。$\Omega_{\mathrm M}$ 为金属中铝原子的摩尔体积，$v$ 为氧化膜内的蠕变速度。采用 Maxwell 黏弹性模型并假定弹性模量很大，可把牛顿流体中的动量平衡写为原式（25），其中 $\eta$ 为黏度。[47]

<a id="S0054"></a>
<a id="S0055"></a>

**S0054 · 源页 7**

<a id="S0054-p1"></a>

**English · 1:** For porous AAO film growing under steady-state, 47 the model predicted that a large compressive interfacial stress causes the lateral flow of oxide materials from the center of pore base toward the cell boundaries and the upward flow in the pore wall oxide, as in the flow pattern experimentally observed fromW tracer studies (see section 6.3.5). Simulation results indicated that the stress field driving the flow results from the following three origins: 'the volume expansion occurring at the metal/oxide interface, nonlinearity of the equations governing conduction of mobile ions (i.e., Al³⁺ and O²⁻ /OH⁻ ), and incorporation of electrolyte-derived anionic species within the anodic oxide near the oxide/electrolyte interface'. 47

**中文：** 对于稳态生长的多孔 AAO，该模型预测：较大的界面压应力会使氧化物从孔底中心横向流向单胞边界，再沿孔壁向上流动。[47] 这一流动图像与钨示踪实验观察到的结果相似，详见第 6.3.5 节。模拟表明，驱动流动的应力场主要有三个来源：金属/氧化物界面处的体积膨胀，控制铝离子和含氧阴离子传导的方程非线性，以及外界面附近氧化物中掺入的电解液来源阴离子。[47]

<a id="S0054-p2"></a>

**English · 2:** For barrier-type anodic alumina film, 48 the model predicted the average stress in the oxide to be compressive when the current density is smaller than 0.5 mA cm⁻² (i.e., compressive to tensile transition at 0.5-1.0 mA cm⁻² ), while average tensile stress of the order of 50 MPa was predicted above 1 mA cm⁻² , which is in good agreement with the experimental stress data of Bradhurst and Leach. 50 In addition, by taking into consideration the viscous flow of oxide material, the model predicted the increases of the cation transport number (t + ) as a function of current density (j). On the basis of experimental evidence that cation transport number (t + ) is largely dependent on the electrolyte condition, Hebert and Houser pointed out that the oxide viscosity and conduction parameters may depend on the solution composition as a result of electrolyte anion incorporation into the anodic oxide film. They suggested that bulky electrolyte anions disrupt the local packing of oxygen ions and influence transport properties by the introduction of additional free volume into the amorphous oxide. 48

**中文：** 对于阻挡型膜，[48]模型预测电流密度低于 0.5 $\mathrm{mA\,cm}^{-2}$ 时，膜内平均应力为压应力；由压应力向拉应力的转变发生在 0.5–1.0 $\mathrm{mA\,cm}^{-2}$ 范围。超过 1 $\mathrm{mA\,cm}^{-2}$ 后，预测的平均拉应力约为 50 MPa，与 Bradhurst 和 Leach 的实验数据较为一致。[50] 考虑黏性流动后，模型还预测阳离子迁移数随电流密度增加而增大。基于迁移数强烈依赖电解液条件的实验事实，Hebert 和 Houser 指出，膜内黏度和传导参数可能因电解液阴离子掺入而随溶液组成变化。他们提出，体积较大的电解液阴离子会扰乱局部氧离子堆积，在非晶氧化物中引入额外自由体积，从而影响输运性质。[48]

<a id="S0056"></a>

<a id="S0056-p1"></a>

### 4. 电解击穿

**English:** 4. ELECTROLYTICBREAKDOWN · [原 PDF 第 7 页](<../原始文件/AAO综述 · 原文.pdf#page=7>)

<a id="S0057"></a>
<a id="S0058"></a>

**S0057 · 源页 7**

<a id="S0057-p1"></a>

**English · 1:** When valve-metals (e.g., Al, Ta, Nb, Zr, etc.) are anodized under galvanostatic conditions, the thickness of the oxide films increases linearly with time. Correspondingly, the applied potential (U) increases linearly with time to keep the electric field (E) constant during the process. Under this condition, the anodizing potential (U) finally reaches a value at which visible sparking on the anode starts appearing, and local thickening, cracking, blistering, or even burning of oxide film commences. This local event is called 'electrolytic breakdown', which not only prevents the uniform growth of anodic films over the macroscopic metal surface, but also permanently degrades the dielectric properties of the oxide. The anodizing potential at the onset of this local event is called breakdown potential (UB). Because the oxide thickness increases linearly with the anodizing potential (U) in galvanostatic conditions, the breakdown is dependent on the oxide thickness and occurs at a critical oxide thickness. Breakdown during anodization can be associated with a number of phenomena. These include the appearance of visible sparking/luminescence, 51-59 the local crystallization of oxide, 60-66 oxygen evolution at the anode, 63,67,68 retardation of potential rise, 69,70 occurrence of audible cracking, 71 and rapid voltage fluctuations. 69,70,72 In porous AAO growth, breakdown can occur under high current density anodizing conditions. 4 If the reaction heat cannot be adequately dissipated from the anode, electrolyte heating may cause local increase in conductivity and a current 'run away' process. This results in local thickening or burning of anodic oxide, terminating uniform growth of porous AAO. The anodic oxide in the burnt area exhibits typically a different color from the burnt-free areas. For a given anodizing electrolyte, on the other hand, porous AAO formed at a potential just below breakdown value (i.e., U < UB) exhibits the best self-ordering of pores (section 7.1). 18 Improving the breakdown characteristics of anodic oxide films through proper control of the electrolyte composition, surface state of the starting aluminum, and reaction heat can allow one not only to explore new anodizing conditions for self-ordered pore growth, but also to engineer internal pore structures (see sections 7.2-7.4). In this section, we discuss some of the electrochemical factors influencing breakdown, and models that explain the breakdown phenomena.

**中文：** 阀金属，如铝、钽、铌和锆，在恒电流条件下阳极氧化时，膜厚随时间线性增加。为保持电场恒定，阳极氧化电位也相应随时间线性升高。最终，当电位达到某一数值时，阳极开始出现可见火花，膜局部增厚、开裂、鼓泡甚至烧蚀。这种局部事件称为“电解击穿”，既妨碍宏观金属表面上的均匀成膜，又会永久损害氧化物的介电性能。开始发生这一事件的电位称为击穿电位 $U_B$。恒电流下膜厚与阳极氧化电位成正比，因此击穿与膜厚有关，并在临界膜厚处发生。击穿可能伴随可见火花或发光、[51–59]局部晶化、[60–66]阳极析氧、[63,67,68]电位上升变缓、[69,70]可听到的开裂声[71]及快速电压波动。[69,70,72] 多孔 AAO 在高电流密度条件下也可能击穿。[4] 若阳极的反应热不能充分散出，电解液升温会使局部电导率提高，引发电流失控，导致局部膜增厚或烧蚀，终止均匀多孔 AAO 的生长。烧蚀区通常与未烧蚀区颜色不同。另一方面，对于给定电解液，在接近但低于击穿电位的条件下形成的多孔 AAO，往往表现出最佳孔自有序程度（见第 7.1 节）。[18] 通过合理控制电解液组成、起始铝表面状态和反应热，改善击穿行为，既可探索新的自有序成孔条件，也可调控内部孔结构，见第 7.2–7.4 节。本节讨论影响击穿的电化学因素及相应解释模型。

<a id="S0059"></a>

<a id="S0059-p1"></a>

### 4.1. 影响击穿的因素

**English:** 4.1. Factors Influencing Breakdown · [原 PDF 第 7 页](<../原始文件/AAO综述 · 原文.pdf#page=7>)

<a id="S0060"></a>
<a id="S0061"></a>

**S0060 · 源页 7、8**

<a id="S0060-p1"></a>

**English · 1:** In general, the breakdown potential (UB) is dependent on the nature of the metal being anodized, the current density (j), and the composition (or resistivity) of the electrolyte. Meanwhile, the electrolyte temperature, stirring rate, and history of anodic oxide have no influence on the breakdown potential.

**中文：** 一般而言，击穿电位取决于被氧化金属的种类、电流密度，以及电解液组成或电阻率。与此同时，电解液温度、搅拌速率和氧化膜历史通常不影响击穿电位。

<a id="S0062"></a>

**S0062 · 源页 8**

<a id="S0062-p1"></a>

**English · 1:** 4.1.1. The Nature of Anodized Metal. Wood and

**中文：** 4.1.1. 被阳极氧化金属的性质。Wood 和

<a id="S0063"></a>

**S0063 · 源页 8**

<a id="S0063-p1"></a>

**English · 1:** Pearson investigated metals whose anodization in 3% ammonium tartrate ended in sparking, and associated the breakdown potential (UB) with the ionic bonding characteristics of the anodic oxides by employing the criteria of Pauling and Wells. They established a descending order of UB according to the melting point of the corresponding oxide: Zr (300 V) > Al (245 V) > Ta (200 V) > Nb (190 V). 72 However, Alwitt and Vijh reported a different descending order of UB for anodizations of the same metals in the same conditions: Al (350 V) > Zr (315 V) > Ta (275) > Nb (190 V). 73 They correlated the increase in UB with the increasing heat of formation per equivalent (-ΔHf/equiv) of oxide, which is approximately equal to one-half the value of the forbidden band gap of the oxide. Further, they noted that the dependence of UB on the band gap would reflect the electronic nature of the breakdown phenomena. As such, rather conflicting reports have been published for the dependence of UB on the intrinsic solidstate properties of anodic oxides. Iknopisov et al. 69 pointed out that the dependence of UB on the nature of the metal is considerably smaller than the dependence on the electrolyte resistivity (ρe).

**中文：** Pearson 研究了在 3% 酒石酸铵溶液中阳极氧化最终出现火花的金属，并用 Pauling 和 Wells 的判据，把击穿电位与氧化物的离子键特征联系起来。他们按相应氧化物的熔点建立了击穿电位的降序排列：Zr（300 V）> Al（245 V）> Ta（200 V）> Nb（190 V）。[72] 但 Alwitt 和 Vijh 在相同条件下研究同类金属时，得到不同排序：Al（350 V）> Zr（315 V）> Ta（275 V）> Nb（190 V）。[73] 他们将击穿电位增大与氧化物每当量生成热 $-\Delta H_f/\mathrm{equiv}$ 的增大联系起来；该生成热近似相当于氧化物禁带宽度的一半，并指出电位对禁带宽度的依赖反映了击穿的电子过程本质。因此，关于击穿电位与氧化物固体本征性质的关系，已有报道相互冲突。Iknopisov 等[69]指出，金属种类对击穿电位的影响明显小于电解液电阻率的影响。

<a id="S0064"></a>
<a id="S0065"></a>

**S0064 · 源页 8**

<a id="S0064-p1"></a>

**English · 1:** 4.1.2. Electrolyte Conditions. Early studies have reported that the breakdown potential (UB) increases linearly with the logarithm of the electrolyte resistivity (ρe) with the following equation: where A and B are the constants depending on the electrolyte composition and the anodized metal. 69,71,74,75 Figure 7 shows the dependence of UB on log ρe during anodization of Nb, Ta, Al, and Zr. 69 It appears from the figure that the different influences of ρe on UB defeat attempts to set the metals in series with respect to the breakdown characteristics of their anodic film. For anodic oxide of aluminum, some authors have reported that anion concentration (CA - ) influences UB. 76,77 Kato et al. showed that at a fixed solution resistivity UB decreases linearly with an increase in the logarithm of the anion concentration, or more specifically the anion charge with the following relation: 77

**中文：** 4.1.2. 电解液条件。早期研究报道，击穿电位与电解液电阻率的对数呈线性关系，见原式（26）。其中，$A$、$B$ 是依赖电解液组成和金属种类的常数。[69,71,74,75] 图 7 给出了 Nb、Ta、Al、Zr 阳极氧化时的相应关系。[69] 各金属对电阻率的响应不同，因此难以根据氧化膜击穿行为给这些金属建立统一排序。对于铝的氧化膜，有研究指出阴离子浓度 $C_{\mathrm A^-}$ 会影响击穿电位。[76,77] Kato 等表明，在溶液电阻率固定时，击穿电位随阴离子浓度，更具体地说随阴离子电荷量的对数增加而线性下降，见原式（27）。[77]

<a id="S0064-p2"></a>

**English · 2:** On the basis of anodization experiments with tantalum in sulfuric, phosphoric, and hydrochloric acids, Arifuku et al. 78 reported that UB is dependent upon the detailed distribution profiles of incorporated anions in the anodic oxide. Later, the role of incorporated electrolyte species in the electrical breakdown was emphasized by Albella et al., who have put forward a theory of avalanche breakdown during anodic oxidation. 79-81

**中文：** Arifuku 等在硫酸、磷酸和盐酸中研究钽阳极氧化后指出，击穿电位依赖膜内掺入阴离子的具体分布。[78] 随后，Albella 等强调了掺入电解液物种的作用，并提出阳极氧化中的雪崩击穿理论。[79–81]

**English · 1:** 4.1.3. Current Density (j). For tantalum anodization in ammonium sulfate, Yahalom and co-workers 76,82 reported that the breakdown potential (UB) is almost independent of the current density (j). For anodic films on aluminum, Ikonopisov et al. 69 also reported that a 500-fold increase of current density (j) only lowers the breakdown potential (UB) by 15%. On the other hand, Di Quarto et al. 74,75,83 pointed out the occurrence of two different kinds of breakdown, that is, 'mechanical' and 'electrical' breakdown. For anodic oxides of tungsten, 74 zirconium, 75 and titanium 84,85 under limited conditions, they noted that anodic oxides grew with an increasing number of defects at a retarded rate (i.e., reduced slope in U-t curve) during galvanostatic anodizations, until electrical breakdown (EB) eventually occurs with visible sparks. They termed this characteristic growth as mechanical breakdown (MB). For electrical breakdown (EB), they reported that current density (j) has little effect on the breakdown potential (UEB), which is in line with the reports of Yahalom et al. and Ikonopisov et al. 69,76,82 In the case of mechanical breakdown (MB), however, they observed that current density (j) has a significant effect on the breakdown potential (UMB) according to the following equation:

**中文：** 4.1.3. 电流密度。对于在硫酸铵中进行的钽阳极氧化，Yahalom 等[76,82]报道击穿电位几乎不随电流密度变化。对铝氧化膜，Ikonopisov 等[69]也发现，电流密度增加 500 倍，仅使击穿电位降低 15%。Di Quarto 等[74,75,83]则区分了“机械击穿”（MB）和“电击穿”（EB）。在一定条件下对钨、[74]锆[75]及钛[84,85]进行恒电流阳极氧化时，膜中缺陷逐渐增加，生长速率减慢，即电位–时间曲线斜率降低，最终才发生伴有可见火花的电击穿。他们把前一种生长行为称为机械击穿。对于电击穿，电流密度对击穿电位的影响很小，与前述研究一致。[69,76,82] 对于机械击穿，电流密度则显著影响 $U_{\mathrm{MB}}$，见原式（28）。

**S0069 · 源页 8、9**

<a id="S0069-p1"></a>

**English · 1:** 4.1.4. Other Factors Influencing Breakdown. The surface state of the starting metal (i.e., the surface defects (flaws), purity, processing history, etc.) also strongly influences the breakdown potential (UB). 61,86 In general, the surface defects unavoidably cause a decrease of the breakdown potential (UB) with the commencement of sparks. 87 On the other hand, post-breakdown anodization experiments have shown that breakdown characteristics are independent of the history of the anodic oxide film. 72,88,89 When a valve-metal was anodized in electrolyte A until breakdown occurred at UB,A, and then the resulting sample was re-anodized in electrolyte B with a higher breakdown potential (UB,B), the film formation during the post-breakdown anodization continued at normal kinetics until breakdown occurred at UB,B. 88,89 Temperature (T) is one of the easily controllable parameters of the electrolyte. Ikonopisov formulated the temperature dependence of the breakdown potential UB (section 4.2.1). 90 However, a change in the electrolyte temperature can alter both the electrolyte resistivity (ρe) and the property of the growing anodic oxide. When the dependence of electrolyte resistivity (ρe) is considered, no clearly pronounced dependence of the breakdown potential (UB) on the temperature (T) was obtained. 71,77,91

**中文：** 4.1.4. 其他影响因素。起始金属的表面缺陷、纯度及加工历史等，也会显著影响击穿电位。[61,86] 通常，表面缺陷会降低开始出现火花的电位。[87] 另一方面，击穿后的再阳极氧化实验表明，击穿特征不依赖原氧化膜的历史。[72,88,89] 例如，先在电解液 A 中氧化至 $U_{B,A}$ 发生击穿，再转入击穿电位更高的电解液 B 中继续氧化，膜可恢复正常生长动力学，直至在 $U_{B,B}$ 再次击穿。[88,89] 温度是容易调控的电解液参数。Ikonopisov 曾建立击穿电位的温度依赖关系，见第 4.2.1 节。[90] 但温度变化也会同时改变电解液电阻率及生长中氧化物的性质。计入电阻率变化后，实验未发现击穿电位对温度具有明显而独立的依赖。[71,77,91]

### 4.2. 击穿模型

**English:** 4.2. Models for Breakdown · [原 PDF 第 9 页](<../原始文件/AAO综述 · 原文.pdf#page=9>)

<a id="S0076"></a>
<a id="S0077"></a>
<a id="S0078"></a>

**S0076 · 源页 9**

<a id="S0076-p1"></a>

**English · 1:** 4.2.1. Electron Avalanche Multiplication. The first attempt to develop a quantitative model of breakdown was made by Iknopisov. 90 He considered experimentally observed breakdown characteristics, and noting that the breakdown potential (UB) mainly depends on the nature of the anodized metal and the electrolyte resistivity (ρe), he inferred that breakdown is dependent upon the solid-state properties of the anodic oxide and is controlled by electrochemical reactions at the oxide/electrolyte interface. In his model, the initial electrons are injected from the electrolyte into the oxide conduction band (CB) by either a Fowler-Nordheim or a Schottky mechanism (Figure 8). The injected electrons accelerate and multiply in avalanche during their travel in the oxide of thickness (tox) to the anode, until the avalanche current reaches a critical value for breakdown. In this multiplication process, the electronic current (je) depends on the travel distance (x) with x = 0 being the oxide/electrolyte interface, which can be expressed by where α(E) is the impact ionization coefficient at the electric field E, r is a recombination constant (r < 1), q is the electron charge, and ψi is the threshold energy for impact ionization. Breakdown occurs if the electronic current (je) exceeds a critical value je,B at a critical oxide thickness (tox,B). Because UB = Etox,B, the breakdown potential (UB) is given by

**中文：** 4.2.1. 电子雪崩倍增。最早尝试建立定量击穿模型的是 Iknopisov。[90] 他根据击穿电位主要取决于金属种类和电解液电阻率的实验事实，推断击穿与氧化物的固体性质有关，并受氧化物/电解液界面电化学反应控制。该模型认为，初始电子通过 Fowler–Nordheim 或 Schottky 机制，从电解液注入氧化物导带（图 8）。注入电子穿过厚度为 $t_{\mathrm{ox}}$ 的氧化膜、移向阳极时，被加速并发生雪崩倍增，直至雪崩电流达到击穿临界值。在倍增过程中，电子电流密度 $j_e$ 随运动距离 $x$ 改变；$x=0$ 对应氧化物/电解液界面，其关系见原式（29）。其中，$\alpha(E)$ 为电场 $E$ 下的碰撞电离系数，$r$ 为复合常数且小于 1，$q$ 为电子电荷，$\psi_i$ 为碰撞电离阈值能量。当电子电流超过临界值 $j_{e,B}$、膜厚达到临界值 $t_{\mathrm{ox},B}$ 时发生击穿。由于 $U_B=Et_{\mathrm{ox},B}$，击穿电位可由原式（30）给出。

<a id="S0076-p2"></a>

**English · 2:** The dependences of je,x=0 on the electric field (E), 69,92 temperature (T), and electrolyte resistivity (ρe) 93 were empirically determined to be, respectively: From eqs 30 and 31, the dependence of breakdown potential (UB) on the electric field (E) is given by This equation explains a slight decrease of UB with increasing current density (j). Regarding the relation between the breakdown potential (UB) and temperature (T), the following expression is obtained by combining eqs 30 and 32:

**中文：** 初始电子电流对电场、[69,92]温度及电解液电阻率[93]的经验依赖分别见原式（31）、（32）、（33）。结合式（30）、（31），可得到击穿电位对电场的依赖，即原式（34）；该式可解释击穿电位随电流密度增加而略微下降。结合式（30）、（32），则得到电位与温度的关系，即原式（35）。

<a id="S0079"></a>

**S0079 · 源页 9**

<a id="S0079-p1"></a>

**English · 1:** Equation 35 predicts that UB is dependent on the temperature (T), which conflicts with experimental observations. 71,77,91 For this discrepancy, Ikonopisov pointed out the interplay between temperature (T) and the solution resistivity (ρe). For the dependence of breakdown potential (UB) on the electrolyte resistivity (ρe), from eqs 30 and 33, one may obtain Equation 36 has exactly the same form as eq 26, which describes an empirical relation between UB and ρe.

**中文：** 式（35）预测击穿电位依赖温度，与部分实验观察不符。[71,77,91] Ikonopisov 将这一差异归因于温度与溶液电阻率之间的耦合。结合式（30）、（33），可得到击穿电位对电阻率的关系，即原式（36），其形式与经验式（26）一致。

<a id="S0079-p2"></a>

**English · 2:** Although Ikonopisov's model explains some of the experimental results, it has been criticized by many authors. Shimizu pointed out the unrealistic value of the mean free path λ(E) of ionized electrons, which from eqs 26, 29, and 36 is given by By using the experimental values from Ikonopisov et al. for E (=8.7 × 10⁶ V cm -1 ) and B/γ2 (=1000 V), Shimizu obtained λ = 500 nm, which roughly corresponds to the thickness of oxide films formed up to the potential 400 V and indicates the absence of the electron avalanche capable of causing the breakdown. 94 Albella et al. questioned the origin of electrons in Ikonopisov's model. 87 They pointed out Ikonopisov's model lacked a reasonable explanation of the role of the electrolyte and the absence of specific electrochemical reactions required for the injection of the initial electrons.

**中文：** 尽管这一模型解释了部分实验结果，仍受到多位研究者批评。Shimizu 指出，由式（26）、（29）、（36）得到的电离电子平均自由程并不现实，计算式见原式（37）。采用实验参数 $E=8.7\times10^6\ \mathrm{V\,cm}^{-1}$、$B/\gamma_2=1000\ \mathrm V$，得到 $\lambda=500\ \mathrm{nm}$，大致相当于在 400 V 下形成的整层氧化膜厚度；这意味着难以发生足以引起击穿的电子雪崩。[94] Albella 等还质疑初始电子的来源：[87]该模型没有合理解释电解液的作用，也没有指出注入初始电子所必需的具体电化学反应。

**S0080 · 源页 9、10**

<a id="S0080-p1"></a>

**English · 1:** Albella et al. explicitly considered the effect of the anodizing electrolyte by posulating that the initial electrons for the avalanche come from the electrolyte species incorporated into anodic oxide. 79-81 The incorporated electrolyte species act as impurity centers close to the oxide conduction band (CB), releasing electrons to the conduction band via the field-assisted Poole-Frenkel mechanism (Figure 9). 80,87,95,96 In the model of Albella et al., the total current density (jt) consists of three components: where j1 is the oxidation current density, j2 is the current density consumed by the incorporated electrolyte species and is assumed to be a constant faction γ of j1 (i.e., j2 = γj1), and je is the electronic current density. The electronic current density (je) at the anode can be expressed as where α is the impact ionization coefficient, and β is the ratio of the oxide thickness to the anodization potential (U) and is equal to the inverse of the electric field E (i.e., β = 1/E = AR, the anodizing ratio). Because the initial electronic current originates from incorporated species, je,x=0 should be a constant fraction η of oxyanion current (j2) and is je,x=0 = ηj2 = ηγj1. Under the assumption that the critical current density is a fraction z of the oxidation current j1, the breakdown potential (UB) should satisfy the following relation:

**中文：** Albella 等明确计入电解液作用，假设雪崩初始电子来自掺入氧化膜的电解液物种。[79–81] 这些物种在靠近导带处形成杂质中心，通过电场辅助的 Poole–Frenkel 机制向导带释放电子（图 9）。[80,87,95,96] 模型将总电流密度分为三部分，见原式（38）：氧化电流 $j_1$；由掺入电解液物种消耗的电流 $j_2$，假设 $j_2=\gamma j_1$，$\gamma$ 为常数；以及电子电流 $j_e$。阳极处电子电流的表达式为原式（39）。其中，$\alpha$ 为碰撞电离系数，$\beta$ 为氧化膜厚与阳极氧化电位的比值，等于电场倒数，也就是阳极氧化比 $AR$。由于初始电子来自掺入物种，初始电子电流应为含氧阴离子电流的固定份额 $\eta$，即 $j_{e,x=0}=\eta j_2=\eta\gamma j_1$。若临界电子电流是氧化电流的份额 $z$，则击穿需满足原式（40），

<a id="S0080-p2"></a>

**English · 2:** Accordingly, the breakdown potential (UB) is given by The time derivative of the potential is given by where ρox is the oxide density, F is the Faraday constant, and M1 and M2 are the molecular weights of the oxide and the incorporated species, respectively, whose corresponding anion and cation valences are x1,y1 and x2,y2, respectively. Combining eqs 38 and 42 yields the following differential equation:

**中文：** 相应电位由原式（41）给出。电位的时间导数见原式（42）：$\rho_{\mathrm{ox}}$ 为氧化物密度，$F$ 为法拉第常数，$M_1$、$M_2$ 分别为氧化物和掺入物种的分子量，其对应阴、阳离子价数为 $x_1,y_1$ 和 $x_2,y_2$。结合式（38）、（42），得到原式（43）的微分方程。

**English · 1:** where K is the unitary rate of anodization for oxide without electrolyte incorporation and given by M1E/x1y1ρoxF, and φ is the ratio of the equivalent weight of the incorporated species to that of oxide, that is, φ = (M2/x2y2)/(M1/x1y1). Assuming a constant field in the oxide, the integration of eq 43 yields the relation between the anodizing potential (U) and time (t): with V(t) and ΔV(t) given by The factor (1 + φγ)/(1 + γ) in eq 45 describes the correction of the anodizing rate due to the incorporation of electrolyte species. On the other hand, eq 46 enforces deviation of potential from the linearity due to the avalanche effect. Accordingly, eq 44 predicts a gradual decrease of slope (dU/ dt) of the potential-time curve during galvanostatic anodization. Albella et al. confirmed the validity of eq 44 by fitting it on the experimental results of tantalum anodization (Figure 10). 80 Further, by fitting eq 44 on the experimental potential evolutions in different electrolyte concentrations (C), they obtained a relation between γ and C: 95

**中文：** 其中，$K$ 为不含掺入物种时的单位阳极氧化速率，表达式为 $M_1E/(x_1y_1\rho_{\mathrm{ox}}F)$；$\varphi$ 为掺入物种与氧化物的当量质量之比，即 $(M_2/x_2y_2)/(M_1/x_1y_1)$。假设膜内电场恒定，对式（43）积分，可得到电位与时间的关系，即原式（44）；$V(t)$、$\Delta V(t)$ 分别由原式（45）、（46）给出。式（45）中的 $(1+\varphi\gamma)/(1+\gamma)$ 修正了物种掺入对成膜速率的影响；式（46）描述电子雪崩使电位偏离线性增长的效应。因此，式（44）预测恒电流氧化时电位–时间曲线斜率逐渐减小。Albella 等用钽阳极氧化实验进行拟合，验证了该式的适用性（图 10）。[80] 进一步拟合不同电解液浓度下的电位演变，得到 $\gamma$ 与浓度 $C$ 的关系，即原式（47）。[95]

**S0087 · 源页 10、11**

<a id="S0087-p1"></a>

**English · 1:** 4.2.2. Stress-Driven Breakdown. Sato 97 distinguished five different possible contributions to the mechanical stresses in anodic oxide: (a) electrostriction pressure, (b) interfacial tension of the film, (c) internal stress caused by the volume expansion, (d) internal stress due to partial hydration/ dehydration of the anodic oxide, and (e) local stress caused by impurities. By considering the first two contributions as the most general factors for breakdown, he mathematically derived a thermodynamic model of stress-driven breakdown, and explained the breakdown potential (UB) and the effect of adsorbed anionic species on it. According to the model, 97 the stress (ΔP) accumulated in the oxide film is equal to where ε is the oxide permittivity and γ is the surface tension. In eq 49, the first term represents the electrostriction effect and the second the interfacial tension effect. According to Sato, for an oxide dielectric with ε > 10, an electric field (E) of 5 × 10⁶ V cm -1 produces a compressive electrostriction pressure exceeding 1000 kg cm⁻² , which is higher than the critical mechanical strength of oxides and thus may be a cause of their mechanical failure. Because the compressive stress within the anodic oxide increases with thickness, there is a limiting oxide thickness (tox,B) above which breakdown occurs. The incorporated anionic species causes a decrease in the surface tension (γ), and thus increases the stress by lowering the value of the second term in eq 49. The dependence of the breakdown potential (UB) on the anion concentration (CA - ) in the electrolyte was also established, as follows:

**中文：** 4.2.2. 应力驱动的击穿。Sato[97]区分了氧化膜机械应力的五个可能来源：电致伸缩压力、膜的界面张力、体积膨胀造成的内应力、局部水合或脱水造成的内应力，以及杂质引起的局部应力。他把前两项视为最普遍的击穿因素，据此推导了应力驱动击穿的热力学模型，并解释了击穿电位及吸附阴离子对它的影响。模型中的膜内累积应力 $\Delta P$ 由原式（49）给出，[97]其中 $\varepsilon$ 为氧化物介电常数，$\gamma$ 为表面张力；第一项表示电致伸缩，第二项表示界面张力。Sato 指出，对于 $\varepsilon>10$ 的介电氧化物，$5\times10^6\ \mathrm{V\,cm}^{-1}$ 的电场可产生超过 1000 $\mathrm{kg\,cm}^{-2}$ 的压缩电致伸缩压力，高于氧化物的临界机械强度，因此可能导致机械失效。由于压应力随膜厚增加，超过临界厚度 $t_{\mathrm{ox},B}$ 后会发生击穿。掺入阴离子会降低表面张力，从而减小式（49）第二项的抵消作用，使应力升高。模型还得到击穿电位对阴离子浓度的依赖关系，即原式（50）。

**S0096 · 源页 11**

<a id="S0096-p1"></a>

**English · 1:** where ΓA - is the anion density at the oxide surface at the breakdown potential (UB). The model predicts a lower breakdown potential for electrolytes having higher anion concentration. Kato et al. have also used electrostriction to explain the enhancement of breakdown by incorporated anionic impurities (see eq 27). 77 They suggested that the incorporated anions lead to additional electrostrictive input into the mechanical stress in oxide films.

**中文：** 其中，$\Gamma_{\mathrm A^-}$ 为达到击穿电位时膜表面的阴离子密度。模型预测，阴离子浓度较高时击穿电位更低。Kato 等也用电致伸缩解释阴离子杂质为何促进击穿，见式（27）：[77]掺入阴离子为膜的机械应力提供了额外电致伸缩贡献。

<a id="S0096-p2"></a>

**English · 2:** Sato noted three different forms of mechanical breakdown depending on the mechanical property of the films: brittle crack for rigid anhydrous anodic oxides, and plastic deformation or flow for visco-plastic hydrous anodic oxides (Figure 11). 97 He suggested that the formation of porous AAO films on aluminum is associated with continuous mechanical breakdown, accompanied by a continuous plastic flow of oxide under high film pressure. The alignment of pores in a close-packed pattern was considered to be a process of relieving the film stress. As the breakdown in the anodic oxidation of aluminum proceeds, therefore, a porous oxide layer progressively forms and thickens on the compact barrier oxide layer, of which thickness remains constant with continuous plastic deformation. This electrostriction-stimulated breakdown model is somewhat in line with the recent flowmodel accounting for the steady-state formation of porous AAO film by Skeldon et al. (see section 6.3.5). 98

**中文：** Sato 根据膜的力学性质区分了三类机械失效：刚性无水氧化膜的脆性开裂，以及黏塑性含水氧化膜的塑性变形或流动（图 11）。[97] 他提出，多孔 AAO 的形成与持续机械失效有关，高膜内压力下氧化物持续发生塑性流动；孔的密堆积排列被视为应力释放过程。因此，随着铝阳极氧化中的这种失效继续，多孔层在致密阻挡层上逐渐形成并增厚，而阻挡层在连续塑性变形中保持固定厚度。这一电致伸缩驱动模型，与 Skeldon 等近年来用来解释稳态多孔 AAO 生长的流动模型存在一定联系，详见第 6.3.5 节。[98]

### 5. 多孔阳极氧化铝（AAO）的结构

**English:** 5. STRUCTUREOFPOROUSANODICALUMINUM OXIDE (AAO) · [原 PDF 第 11 页](<../原始文件/AAO综述 · 原文.pdf#page=11>)

<a id="S0099"></a>

<a id="S0099-p1"></a>

### 5.1. 基本结构

**English:** 5.1. General Structure · [原 PDF 第 11 页](<../原始文件/AAO综述 · 原文.pdf#page=11>)

<a id="S0100"></a>
<a id="S0101"></a>

**S0100 · 源页 11**

<a id="S0100-p1"></a>

**English · 1:** Figure 12 shows schematically an idealized structure of porous AAO, together with scanning electron microscopy (SEM) images of each part of the porous AAO. Porous AAO has a honeycomb-like structure. Porous oxide layer formed on aluminum substrate contains a large number of mutually parallel pores. Each cylindrical nanopore and its surrounding oxide constitute a hexagonal cell aligned normal to the metal surface. Each nanopore at the metal/oxide interface is closed by a thin barrier oxide layer with an approximately hemispherical morphology. Under proper anodization conditions, the oxide cells are self-organized to form a hexagonally close-packed structure. 7 On the other hand, the surface of the aluminum after complete removal of the porous oxide layer is textured with arrays of concave features. The thickness of the porous AAO layer on aluminum is proportional to the total charge (Qc) involved in the electrochemical oxidation. Therefore, the depth of oxide nanopores is easily tunable from a few tens of nanometers up to hundreds of micrometers by controlling anodization time (t). In general, the structure of self-ordered porous AAO is often defined by several structural parameters, such as interpore distance (Dint), pore diameter (Dp), barrier layer thickness (tb), pore wall thickness (tw), pore density (ρp), and porosity (P). For ideally ordered porous AAO, the following relationships can be drawn by simple geometric consideration:

**中文：** 图 12 示意了多孔AAO 的理想结构，并展示各部位的 SEM 图像。多孔 AAO 具有蜂窝状结构，铝基底上的多孔氧化层含有大量彼此平行的孔。一个圆柱形纳米孔及其周围氧化物共同构成垂直于金属表面的六边形单胞。每个孔在金属/氧化物界面处由近似半球形的薄阻挡层封闭。在适当阳极氧化条件下，单胞自组织为六方密堆积结构。[7] 完全去除多孔膜后，铝表面留下凹坑阵列。多孔层厚度与电化学氧化通过的总电荷 $Q_c$ 成正比，因而可通过控制阳极氧化时间，把孔深从数十纳米调节至数百微米。描述自有序多孔 AAO 的常用结构参数包括孔间距 $D_{\mathrm{int}}$、孔径 $D_p$、阻挡层厚度 $t_b$、孔壁厚度 $t_w$、孔密度 $\rho_p$ 和孔隙率 $P$。对于理想有序结构，简单几何分析可得到原式（51）至（53）的关系。

<a id="E0051"></a>

**原式（51） / Eq. (51)** · [原 PDF 第 11 页](<../原始文件/AAO综述 · 原文.pdf#page=11>)

#### 图 12 / Figure 12 · 源页 12

![Figure 12](assets/013.png)

**English caption:** Figure 12. Schematic structure of (a) porous anodic aluminum oxide (AAO) on Al foil and (b) cross-sectional view. (c−e) SEM images of porous AAO, showing top surface, barrier layer, and bottom surface, respectively. Scale bars are 1 μm. Panels c−e were reprinted with permission from ref 111. Copyright 2006 Macmillan Publishers Ltd.: Nature Materials.

**中文图注：** 图 12. 铝箔上多孔 AAO 的结构（a）和截面（b）示意；（c–e）SEM 分别显示上表面、阻挡层与底面，标尺均为 1 μm。原文注明 c–e 经许可转载自文献 [111]，版权 2006 Macmillan Publishers Ltd.: Nature Materials。

<a id="S0102"></a>

**S0102 · 源页 11**

<a id="S0102-p1"></a>

**English · 1:** These structural parameters of porous AAO are known to be dependent on the anodizing conditions: the type of electrolyte, anodizing potential (U), current density (j), temperature (T), etc. Among those, anodizing potential (U) and current density (j) are the most important electrochemical parameters. A review on this matter has recently been published by Sulka. 99 Here, we briefly discuss the major structural parameters of porous AAO and the electrochemical factors influencing them.

**中文：** 这些参数取决于电解液种类、阳极氧化电位、电流密度和温度等条件，其中电位与电流密度最为重要。Sulka 最近对此作了综述。[99] 下文简要讨论主要结构参数及其电化学影响因素。

**S0103 · 源页 11、12**

<a id="S0103-p1"></a>

**English · 1:** 5.1.1. Pore Diameter (Dp). O'Sullivan and Wood used electron microscopy to quantitatively study the morphology of porous AAO potentiostatically formed in phosphoric acid (H3PO4) electrolyte. 100 The pore diameter (Dp), interpore distance (Dint), and barrier layer thickness (tb) were observed to be directly proportional to the anodizing potential (U). Their microscopic analysis revealed that the pore diameter increases at the rate (ζp) of 1.29 nm V⁻¹ with respect to the anodizing potential (U): They pointed out that the electrolyte concentration does not significantly influence the pore diameter (Dp), while the temperature of the electrolyte is positively correlated with pore diameter. 100 On the other hand, theoretical modeling of porous AAO growth performed by Parkutik and Shershulsky predicted a decrease in pore diameter with decreasing electrolyte pH (i.e., increasing electrolyte concentration) due to the enhanced dissolution velocity of anodic oxide at the pore base. 101 Moreover, a recent study by Sulka and Parkoła indicated that the pore diameter decreases with decreasing temperature. 102 In general, pore diameter close to the surface of a porous AAO film is larger than that close to the pore bottoms (i.e., truncated pore channels), especially when anodization is conducted at an elevated temperature and/or for an extended period of time. This can be attributed to the chemical dissolution of the pore wall oxide by acid electrolyte.

**中文：** 5.1.1. 孔径。O’Sullivan 和 Wood 采用电子显微技术，定量研究了磷酸电解液中恒电位制备多孔 AAO 的形貌。[100] 他们发现，孔径、孔间距及阻挡层厚度均与电位成正比；孔径随电位增加的速率为 $\zeta_p=1.29\ \mathrm{nm\,V}^{-1}$，见原式（54）。他们指出，电解液浓度对孔径影响不显著，而温度与孔径正相关。[100] Parkutik 和 Shershulsky 的生长模型则预测：电解液 pH 降低，即浓度增加，会增强孔底氧化物溶解，导致孔径减小。[101] Sulka 和 Parkoła 的较近期研究也表明，温度降低会使孔径减小。[102] 一般而言，膜表面附近的孔径大于孔底附近，孔道因此呈截锥状；这一现象在温度较高或氧化时间较长时更明显，原因是酸性电解液对孔壁的持续化学溶解。

<a id="E0054"></a>

**原式（54） / Eq. (54)** · [原 PDF 第 12 页](<../原始文件/AAO综述 · 原文.pdf#page=12>)

![原式（54）截图](assets/equations/E0054.png)

**S0109 · 源页 12、13**

<a id="S0109-p1"></a>

**English · 1:** Accordingly, pore diameter measured from the pore bottom (not from the surface of AAO film) is more relevant for investigating the intrinsic effect of electrochemical parameters on the structure of porous AAO.

**中文：** 因此，研究电化学参数对原生结构的影响时，测量孔底而非膜表面的孔径更有意义。

<a id="S0109-p2"></a>

**English · 2:** Recently, Lee et al. 103 reported that pore diameter (Dp) increases with current density (j) under potentiostatic anodization conditions (Figure 13). Under specific experimental conditions, they observed spontaneously oscillating current during potentiostatic hard anodization (HA) at the potential range of 140-200 V (see section 7.2). They suggested that at a given potentiostatic condition (i.e., U = constant) the distribution of the current lines and electric field (E) may be sensitively varied by the geometric details of the barrier oxide layer. That would influence the movement rates of the electrolyte/oxide and oxide/metal interfaces, hence the pore diameter (Dp). 103

**中文：** Lee 等最近报道，在恒电位条件下，孔径随电流密度增加而增大（图 13）。[103] 在特定条件下，他们观察到 140–200 V 恒电位硬阳极氧化（HA）过程中出现自发电流振荡，见第 7.2 节。他们认为，即使电位固定，阻挡层的具体几何形貌仍可敏感地改变电流线和电场分布，进而影响电解液/氧化物及氧化物/金属界面的移动速率，最终改变孔径。[103]

<a id="S0118"></a>
<a id="S0119"></a>

**S0117 · 源页 13**

<a id="S0117-p1"></a>

**English · 1:** 5.1.2. Interpore Distance (Dint). It has long been established that the interpore distance (Dint) is also linearly proportional to the anodizing potential (U). 7,100,104-109 A detailed study on this matter was performed for sulfuric and oxalic acid by Ebihara et al. 104,105 Their empirical expressions on the relationship between the interpore distance (Dint) and anodizing potential (U) are as follows: For oxalic acid-based anodizations in the potential range of 20–60 V, Hwang et al. reported that interpore distance only depends on anodizing potential (U), not on the temperature of the electrolyte: 107 This temperature independence of the interpore distance is in line with the results of Keller et al., 7 but conflicts with the experimental results of Sulka and Parkoła, 102 who observed that interpore distance is positively correlated with temperature for self-ordered porous AAOs formed by sulfuric acid-based anodization; interpore distance at an elevated temperature (10 °C) is about 10% larger than that at a low temperature (i.e., -8 to 1 °C). O'Sullivan and Wood 100 reported for phosphoric acid-based anodization that increasing the temperature or the electrolyte concentration decreases the interpore distance. For self-ordered porous AAOs formed by mild anodization (MA) conditions using sulfuric, oxalic, and phosphoric acid, it has generally been accepted that the interpore distance (Dint) is linearly proportional to the anodizing potential (U) with a proportionality constant ζMA of 2.5 nm V⁻¹ (see section 7.1): 109 However, this empirical formula is not valid for hard anodization (HA), under conditions in which a high electric field (E) is exerted across the barrier layer due to high current density (j) during anodization. 16,110-113 This will be discussed in detail in section 7.2.

**中文：** 5.1.2. 孔间距。长期以来已确认，孔间距也与阳极氧化电位近似线性相关。[7,100,104–109] Ebihara 等对硫酸和草酸体系作了详细研究，所得经验关系见原式（55）至（57）。[104,105] 对于 20–60 V 的草酸阳极氧化，Hwang 等报道孔间距只取决于电位，不取决于电解液温度，见原式（58）。[107] 这一温度独立性与 Keller 等[7]的结果一致，但与 Sulka 和 Parkoła 的硫酸体系实验不同：[102]后者发现，10 °C 下形成的自有序 AAO 孔间距，比 −8 至 1 °C 下大约 10%。O’Sullivan 和 Wood 则报道，磷酸体系中提高温度或电解液浓度会减小孔间距。[100] 对于硫酸、草酸及磷酸中采用温和阳极氧化（MA）形成的自有序 AAO，通常接受孔间距与电位成正比，比例常数 $\zeta_{\mathrm{MA}}$ 约为 2.5 $\mathrm{nm\,V}^{-1}$，见原式（59）及第 7.1 节。[109] 然而，这一经验式不适用于高电流密度、阻挡层电场较强的硬阳极氧化条件。[16,110–113] 第 7.2 节将进一步讨论。

<a id="S0121"></a>

**S0120 · 源页 13**

<a id="S0120-p1"></a>

**English · 1:** 5.1.3. Barrier Layer Thickness (tb). The thickness of the barrier layer (tb) is one of the most important structural parameters of porous AAO for understanding the kinetics of the electrochemical oxidation of aluminum. Like other structural parameters, barrier layer thickness (tb) is also dependent on the anodizing potential (U). The potential dependence of the barrier layer thickness has also been known as 'anodizing ratio (AR = tb/U)', the inverse of which corresponds to the electric field (E) across the barrier layer, and it determines the ionic current density (j) (see eq 1). Accordingly, at a given anodizing potential (U), current density (j) increases exponentially as a function of the inverse of the anodizing ratio AR (i.e., the electric field strength E). Earlier studies have indicated that the anodizing ratio equals 1.2 nm V⁻¹ . 7,100,114 For self-ordered porous AAOs formed by mild anodization (MA, see section 7.1) in oxalic and phosphoric acid at various anodizing potentials (U), Vrublevsky et al. have reported empirical equations based on results from their reanodizing experiments. 115-117 They used the equations for estimating the barrier layer thickness by assuming the anodizing ratio, ARMA = 1.14 nm V⁻¹ . On the other hand, reduced anodizing ratios for sulfuric and oxalic acid have recently been reported for hard anodization (HA); ARHA = 0.6-1.0 nm V⁻¹ (see Figure 13d and section 7.2). 18,111,113,118,119 Chu et al. 18 determined the anodizing ratio for less-popular anodizing electrolytes (e.g., tartaric, citric, glycolic, and malic acids) by performing what they termed 'critical-potential anodization'.

**中文：** 5.1.3. 阻挡层厚度。孔底阻挡层厚度是理解铝电化学氧化动力学的重要结构参数，也依赖阳极氧化电位。二者之比称为“阳极氧化比” $AR=t_b/U$，其倒数对应阻挡层电场，并通过式（1）决定离子电流密度。因此，电位给定时，电流密度随 $AR$ 的倒数，即电场强度，呈指数增加。早期研究给出的 $AR$ 为 1.2 $\mathrm{nm\,V}^{-1}$。[7,100,114] Vrublevsky 等对不同电位下、草酸或磷酸中温和阳极氧化制备的自有序膜开展再阳极氧化实验，并给出经验关系；估算阻挡层厚度时采用 $AR_{\mathrm{MA}}=1.14\ \mathrm{nm\,V}^{-1}$。[115–117] 较近期的硬阳极氧化研究则在硫酸和草酸中得到较小值，$AR_{\mathrm{HA}}=0.6$–$1.0\ \mathrm{nm\,V}^{-1}$，见图 13d 及第 7.2 节。[18,111,113,118,119] Chu 等还用所谓“临界电位阳极氧化”，研究了酒石酸、柠檬酸、乙醇酸和苹果酸等较少使用的电解液。[18]

<a id="S0122"></a>

**S0122 · 源页 13**

<a id="S0122-p1"></a>

**English · 1:** The anodizing ratio for various electrolytes was determined to be AR ≈ 1 nm V⁻¹ (Figure 14). It should be noted that this value of anodizing ratio is the averaged proportionality constant determined from mild and hard anodization experiments of aluminum.

**中文：** 不同电解液中得到的平均阳极氧化比约为 1 $\mathrm{nm\,V}^{-1}$（图 14）。需要注意，该值是综合温和与硬阳极氧化实验后得到的平均比例常数。

<a id="Figure-14"></a>
<a id="C0014"></a>
